### V3 bounded enrichment execution

An explicit -ExecuteValueEnrichment option now requires SAFE_REFRESH plus
-BuildValueResearchPriority. It invokes the existing queue CLI with an execution

its bound original inputs, downloads only a registered official annual URL with
an expected hash through the existing bounded PDF adapter, caches immutable
originals, extracts candidates and runs the unchanged quality scorer. Each issuer

coverage, admitted fact persistence or completion of deep research.

### 2026-10-26 reusable owner-cash valuation module scope

Current implementation scope is deliberately limited to one reusable chain:
operating facts and hypotheses -> owner-accessible cash and constrained
distribution -> annual equity changes -> existing residual-income/distribution
valuation -> existing research candidate, API and Web consumers. The frozen
five-year joint-capital implementation and the existing 600519 product-specific
ten-year evidence remain immutable historical inputs; neither will be renamed or
rewritten as the new generic contract.

The new versioned contract must contain no security, product, subsidiary or fund
names. Company differences stay in source-bound inputs and low-confidence
research assumptions. 600519 is the first economic case. 600887 is fixed as the
second migration case because it already has FY2025/H1-2026 original filings,
parent/consolidated cash facts, distributions and an existing supported quality
compounder package. 000568 remains unsuitable for this acceptance because its
current evidence set cannot yet produce a successful, recomputable second path;
an UNKNOWN-only result would test fail-closed behavior, not reuse.

Allowed code changes are the versioned ten-year owner-cash kernel, the existing
neutral proposal and pending-candidate dispatch, the existing economic review
projection, and the existing joint-capital Web component. Required inputs include
explicit cash scope, cash floor, distribution capacity, accessible-profit ratio,
maintenance cash, working-capital cash, commitments, reserves, target payout and
non-silent terminal continuation. Actual distribution is constrained rather than
forced. The existing residual-income path with annual retentions remains the only
valuation consumer; no second valuation or price engine is authorized.

Acceptance requires two real, source-bound companies through the same entry and
shared formulas, different annual paths and conclusions, candidate replay, API/Web
consumption, exact update invalidation and fail-closed scope rejection. No G3,
current-price admission, personal quantity or order is implied.

ledger -> annual equity path -> existing residual-income path-v2 -> candidate,

### 2026-10-26 v3 terminal economics and portable two-company closure

The reusable owner-cash module now uses `joint-capital-cash-path-v3` and an
explicit `terminal-economic-contract-v1`. Terminal value no longer converts all
year-10 retained book into the operating franchise ROE. The contract separately
states continuing operating profit, maintenance/WC cash needs, accessible cash,
terminal distribution, low-return liquidity and residual convergence. Missing
terminal economics is rejected; v2 remains available only for historical replay.

New v3 candidates retain repository code/config/docs by content hash and rewrite
their local evidence locators to the candidate's immutable closure. Repeated
source identities are restored once. A later checkout or assumptions update can
therefore verify the sealed research bytes without silently interpreting them
through current source. Source tampering, a missing retained blob or a mismatched
hash still fails closed. This is evidence retention only: retained Python is
never imported or executed.

The closure now also seals the predecessor research package and economic-policy
inputs themselves. Their local `path`/`local_path` references are rebound to the
same content-addressed closure while immutable public source URLs remain
unchanged. A candidate therefore remains independently verifiable after the
original package or policy file drifts; it does not weaken source identity or
silently consume the newer bytes.

### 2026-10-27 - F1/E1/G1 v4 economic-terminal first batch

Scope is limited to valuation-kernel correctness, existing 600519/600887
candidate generation/replay/consumption, and the existing API/Web display. No
Agent, hotspot, third-company, approval, account, scheduler or order work is part
of this batch.

F1 adds `joint-capital-cash-path-v4` and
`residual-income-equity-path-v4` without changing the historical v1/v2/v3
implementations. The v4 model calculates annual clean-surplus residual income
directly and no longer calls the legacy terminal-growth helper. Terminal value is
accepted only for an explicitly supported exact economic tail after all declared
finite events. It reports the analytical method, last scheduled event and a zero
analytical-tail truncation bound. Finite-precision bridge tolerance is reported
separately, while economic-assumption uncertainty is explicitly not quantified by
that bound; unsupported or non-convergent tails fail closed. T01-T07
cover low-payout diagnostic growth above cost, an independent full-distribution
perpetuity, a 16% stable distribution benchmark, scheduled-event ordering,
unsupported tails, legacy byte stability and invalid/future inputs.

E1 reuses the existing neutral proposal, descriptor, RunSpec,
ResearchApplicationService, review packet and pending-candidate consumer. A
single current command now builds and verifies both retained-source candidates;
it can publish them to the existing research-only pointer but cannot approve
research, admit price, calculate quantity or place an order. Explicit real-source
replay with `JOINT_CASH_V4_REAL_REQUIRED=1` passed for both issuers. Conditional
per-share values are:

- 600519: bear/base/bull 675.1019318083 / 799.2027611908 /
  930.0062820281 CNY, retained valuation basis 2026-06-30.
- 600887: bear/base/bull 13.0011250922 / 16.0892188495 /
  20.2405823121 CNY, retained valuation date 2026-10-21.

Both are LOW-confidence `conditional_research_only` results, with
`research_admitted=false`, `price_admitted=false`, `position_guidance=null` and
`action=no_order`. They are not current fair-value or buy claims.

G1 fixes two actual product breaks: a valid registered research candidate was
hidden when the separate pinned public publication chain was unavailable, and an
older company/opportunity row could continue to expose a price plan bound to a
superseded research descriptor. The decision-detail endpoint now preflights the
registered candidate first. A valid latest candidate is the displayed research
analysis; a stale price plan is withheld, while a broken explicitly registered
candidate fails closed instead of falling through to old numbers. The existing
company page accepts the standalone `research_analysis` field and renders the same
v4 joint-capital review without inventing a price plan. The Web validator now
recognizes terminal-contract-v2, distinguishes analytical truncation from economic
uncertainty, and labels `terminal_growth` as a non-authoritative accounting
diagnostic rather than a v4 valuation sensitivity.

The existing economic-assessment and review-material path now dispatches the v4
retention contract through the v4 input adapter and v4 valuation arithmetic. It
no longer diagnoses a v4 candidate through the legacy Gordon-growth helper, and
its cost/terminal review group describes the explicit owner-cash tail contract
rather than presenting diagnostic book-retention growth as an authoritative
terminal-growth assumption.
